# Workflow specifications

All cards describe proposed behavior; referenced owner directions remain as identified in [answers](answers.md). These are specifications, not implemented state machines or executed tests. Every transition uses an authenticated eligible actor or the expressly limited applicant/guest route; server-side scope/revision/idempotency checks remain required even when controls are hidden.

## Shared transaction and timing contract

A meaningful state transition has one operation ID, expected source state/revision, exact owning parish/record, actual actor and responsible authority. Retry first queries that operation; a lost response means outcome unknown until checked. Commit primary facts atomically; a failed secondary attachment/notification creates persistent partial-result status and retries only that component. Internal instruction time, original historical event time and actual system entry time remain distinct.

Every card inherits [proposed operational defaults](open-inputs-and-conflicts.md#operational-defaults). Office worklists are reviewed once each working day; an overdue task routes to its named lead, then competent priest/authority. This is an internal proposal, not a promised applicant SLA. No background reminders/expiry messages are described as delivered without a real runner. Expiry/permission/availability eligibility is evaluated again on protected actions even if a reminder was missed.

Terminal results cannot use ordinary cancel/delete. Their correction/reversal/withdrawal-of-access procedure must preserve the original. A returned item resumes review; it never revives an invalid signature, prior capacity hold or old grant. Where a card includes a reactivation/reopen cycle, the new cycle gets fresh checks and audit.

## WF01

**Create an individual account** — S06. Detailed field binding: [FM01](field-dictionary.md#fm01).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Visitor selects Create account from public Sign in. |
| Data | Supplied given/family name, exact birth date for product eligibility, email and/or phone, password confirmation, language and notices. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Draft > Submitted > Account active. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Account holder; security support handles failures. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Under-age applicants use verified guardian-assisted person/event records; ambiguous contacts use neutral recovery. Missing verification remains Unverified, not fake success. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf01); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | One individual account and own combined home, with skippable profile completion. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF01-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF01-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF01-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF01-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF01-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

| Transition | Source → action → destination | Allowed actor/scope | Prerequisite and confirmation | Committed result and audit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| WF01-T01 | Draft → Record Submitted → Submitted | Applicant; exact card scope | Age-policy review complete; unique supported sign-in identity; real successful save; no staff/person record created. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Submitted for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF01-T01 once. |
| WF01-T02 | Submitted → Record Account active → Account active | Identity service; exact card scope | Age-policy review complete; unique supported sign-in identity; real successful save; no staff/person record created. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Account active for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF01-T02 once. |
| WF01-T90 | Draft / Submitted → Request correction / Return for information → Returned / Needs information | Account holder; security support handles failures. (eligible reviewer for this exact stage); exact card scope | Only fields still legally/operationally reviewable at the selected listed source state; identify exact field/document and saved resume state. No return action overwrites signed/posted/official facts; those use amendment/reversal. Separate applicant explanation from private notes. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Keep entered facts and prior decisions; assign supplier; record required item, useful reason and exact prior review state to resume. |
| WF01-T91 | Returned / Needs information → Submit corrected information → Prior review stage, rechecked | Permitted applicant/supplier or attributed office recorder; exact card scope | Revalidate supplied fields/documents, authority and revision; correction cannot inherit an invalid signature or stale consent. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Preserve previous version; reopen only applicable review; notify exact current owner. |
| WF01-T92 | Draft / Submitted → Withdraw / Decline / Cancel → Withdrawn / Declined / Cancelled | Applicant for unhandled own request only; otherwise Account holder; security support handles failures. within deciding authority; exact card scope | Choose actual outcome and reason; preview dependent holds, payments, files and people. Cancelling an unperformed request never cancels actual receipt/payment/celebration. Terminal/official/posted facts use their separate review/correction flow. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Stop future new work/reminders; release only unneeded provisional resources; retain history; create explicit refund/review task rather than claim money returned. |
| WF01-T93 | Account active / Cancelled / Returned, only where reviewed correction is permitted → Request reviewed reopening / correction → Reopened review, not restored approval | Account holder; security support handles failures. and actual competent approver; exact card scope | Permission, retention and domain policy permit; signed/official/posted results are amended or replaced, never erased; recheck current availability/eligibility. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | New attributed history and current owner; prior outcomes stay visible; downstream approvals require their own fresh conditions. |

## WF02

**Recover credentials and manage sessions** — S06. Detailed field binding: [FM02](field-dictionary.md#fm02).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Account holder chooses Forgot password, Change password or Lost device in My account. |
| Data | Own identifier, actual verified channel or recorded manual identity review; recovery token; current password for authenticated changes; selected session IDs. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Requested > Identity checked > Recovery authorized > Credentials changed > Other sessions revoked > Closed. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Security support; ordinary office only for eligible nonstaff recovery. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Unknown contact yields neutral response. No usable channel uses reviewed manual recovery. Lost response checks actual reset state; never email an invented password. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf02); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Holder can authenticate; revoked sessions fail their next protected request; minimal recovery audit retained. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF02-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF02-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF02-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF02-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF02-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

| Lifecycle | Prepared > Link created > Shared manually or sent > Claimed > Linked. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. ## WF04

**Follow, join and leave a parish** — S07. Detailed field binding: [FM04](field-dictionary.md#fm04).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Signed-in user chooses Follow, Set primary or Request membership on a published parish. |
| Data | Target parish, relationship request, supplied distinguishing facts and minimal representation/identity evidence. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Membership requested > Under identity review > Linked > Departure requested > Membership ended. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Parish office, falling back to assigned priest/Parish Admin. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Ambiguous/archive/already-linked candidates wait for evidence. Decline gives useful reason. Leaving/unfollowing does not move records or revoke unrelated appointments. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf04); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Dated parish relationship; permitted historical request tracking retained. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF04-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF04-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF04-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF04-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF04-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

| Transition | Source → action → destination | Allowed actor/scope | Prerequisite and confirmation | Committed result and audit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| WF04-T01 | Membership requested → Record Under identity review → Under identity review | Office reviewer; exact card scope | Following and primary selection are immediate preferences. Membership link requires office identity check; no self-review; departure previews open work. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Under identity review for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF04-T01 once. |
| WF04-T02 | Under identity review → Record Linked → Linked | Office reviewer; exact card scope | Following and primary selection are immediate preferences. Membership link requires office identity check; no self-review; departure previews open work. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Linked for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF04-T02 once. |
| WF04-T03 | Linked → Record Departure requested → Departure requested | Member; exact card scope | Following and primary selection are immediate preferences. Membership link requires office identity check; no self-review; departure previews open work. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Departure requested for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF04-T03 once. |
| WF04-T04 | Departure requested → Record Membership ended → Membership ended | Office reviewer; exact card scope | Following and primary selection are immediate preferences. Membership link requires office identity check; no self-review; departure previews open work. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Membership ended for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF04-T04 once. |
| WF04-T90 | Membership requested / Under identity review / Linked / Departure requested → Request correction / Return for information → Returned / Needs information | Parish office, falling back to assigned priest/Parish Admin. (eligible reviewer for this exact stage); exact card scope | Only fields still legally/operationally reviewable at the selected listed source state; identify exact field/document and saved resume state. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | New attributed history and current owner; prior outcomes stay visible; downstream approvals require their own fresh conditions. |

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |

Do not duplicate a person to model secondary residence. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf08); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Accurate dated relationships and contact defaults, with accessible relationship list and purpose-scoped representation. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF08-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF08-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF08-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF08-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF08-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF09

## WF13

**Reserve a facility** — S23. Detailed field binding: [FM13](field-dictionary.md#fm13).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Requester selects free/busy resource and submits a booking request. |
| Data | Resource, dates/time zone, setup/cleanup, purpose, attendees, responsible adult, equipment, approved prices/deposit terms and access needs. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Draft > Requested > Office reviewed > Tentative hold > Confirmed > In use > Completed. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Resource manager; priest is default confirmation authority. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Competing request becomes Alternative needed, not auto-refused. Maintenance/schedule changes need reviewed relocation and applicant notices. Cancellation/refund remain separate. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf13); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | One confirmed reservation/canonical occurrence; custody/access and financial close tasks recorded. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF13-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF13-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF13-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF13-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF13-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF16

**Confirmation/chrismation preparation and registration** — S12 S13. Detailed field binding: [FM16](field-dictionary.md#fm16).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Applicant/office chooses only the jurisdiction-approved separate or baptism-linked option. |
| Data | Identity, rite/jurisdiction, baptism reference, approved terminology/checklist, sponsor/formation where required, actual celebrant/date. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Received > Rite route verified > Priest accepted > Prepared > Scheduled > Celebrated > Entry approved. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Competent clergy policy owner and responsible priest. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Unsupported route goes to clergy review. Previously recorded reception is referenced, never duplicated by naming differences. Corrections use WF19. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf16); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Only the approved rite route and actual celebration produce an official entry. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF16-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF16-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF16-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF16-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF16-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF17

**Marriage preparation, celebration and registration** — S12 S13. Detailed field binding: [FM17](field-dictionary.md#fm17).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | One/both partners or office initiate a marriage request. |
| Data | Both partners, supplied official names, separate contact/authority, external parish/celebrant, witnesses, prior evidence, special permission references and resources. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Received > Priest review > External/special authorization > Preparation complete > Scheduled > Celebrated > Entry submitted > Registered. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Assigned priest; competent ecclesiastical authority for exceptional route. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Clergy/room conflicts require alternatives. Partner privacy remains separate. Postponement/cancellation updates linked certificate/booking/payment work; never fabricate a celebration. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf17); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Actual reviewed marriage entry with source permissions and immutable issued snapshot. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF17-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF17-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF17-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF18

**Arrange a funeral and record its outcome** — S13. Detailed field binding: [FM18](field-dictionary.md#fm18).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Family/representative uses Funeral request plus maintained urgent pastoral contact. |
| Data | Deceased identity, reported death facts, authorized contact, timing, location, celebrant and approved urgent logistics/checklist. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Received urgent > Priest accepted > Arrangements confirmed > Service occurred > Outcome reviewed > Closed. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Signing priest; bishop review owner; office preparation lead. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Returned signed content creates new revision, invalidates its material signature and requires resign/review. Legacy issued lacks invented acceptance. No certificate validity period invented. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf20); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | One versioned issuance authorized by actual applicable signatures/acceptance, ready for separately verified release. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF20-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF20-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF20-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF20-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF20-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

**Release, download or collect an authorized document** — S14 S15. Detailed field binding: [FM21](field-dictionary.md#fm21).

Clergy/bishop/admin access is field/action-specific; leadership, rank and combined views do not broaden it. Search/exports/counts apply the same restrictions. |
| Ownership and time | One plan coordinator; contributors see only assigned audience. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Unsaved close offers Save/Discard/Stay. Concurrent edits reconcile. Template edits do not mutate saved plans; fixed start constraints shown with gaps/overlaps. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf24); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Versioned running sheet and distinct clergy/team/participant prints, no unrelated private notes. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF24-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF24-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF24-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF24-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF24-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF29

**Borrow and return group resources** — S19. Detailed field binding: [FM29](field-dictionary.md#fm29).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Permitted member/leader selects physical resource request; digital access uses audience directly. |
| Data | Item/quantity/condition, group owner, borrower/contact basis, due date, issue/return and damage evidence. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Requested > Approved > Issued > Returned > Condition checked > Closed. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Assigned owner/operators see exact permitted parish/group/record scope. Applicant/guardian receives only their own authorized projection if involved. Clergy/bishop/admin access is field/action-specific; leadership, rank and combined views do not broaden it. Search/exports/counts apply the same restrictions. |
| Ownership and time | Group/resource custodian. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Overdue/lost/damaged goes to follow-up and WF37 repair/finance as approved. Leader handover transfers custody, never personal notes. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf29); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Quantity and condition reconcile with stock; dated loan history and restricted borrower details. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF29-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF29-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF29-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF29-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF29-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF30

**Offer, confirm and complete volunteer duties** — S20. Detailed field binding: [FM30](field-dictionary.md#fm30).

| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Eligible person opens opportunity or coordinator sends an invitation for a defined slot. |

| Lifecycle | Invited or offered > Awaiting confirmation > Confirmed > Served > Hours submitted > Hours approved. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. WF32-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF33

**Record and allocate an actual receipt** — S21 S25. Detailed field binding: [FM33](field-dictionary.md#fm33).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Authorized cashier/event/finance operator records genuine cash/bank/OMT receipt or claimed transfer awaiting verification. |
| Data | Payer, original currency/amount/date/method/reference, source case, allocations, exchange basis if actually converted and operator. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Claim reported > Receipt verified > Transaction posted > Allocated > Receipt acknowledged. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Entry/approval/payment/reconciliation roles see only granted financial scope; donor identity is a separate grant. Applicant/payer sees own permitted receipts/balance; guardian/household adult has no automatic donor access. Leaders and ordinary office have no full Finance projection. |
| Ownership and time | Finance operator and independent reviewer for corrections. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Overpayment remains unapplied credit/refund task; split participants use explicit allocation; repeated import/retry deduplicates source reference. Corrections reverse, not overwrite. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf33); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Posted amount reconciles to allocations plus unapplied remainder; purpose screens show the same movement. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF33-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF33-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF33-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF33-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF33-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF34

**Check a participant into the exact event** — S22. Detailed field binding: [FM34](field-dictionary.md#fm34).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Assigned door operator selects event/occurrence/site and searches authorized registration. |
| Data | Participant/registration, consent/eligibility, permitted alerts, arrival time, operator and active session. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Expected > Identity checked > Checked in > Checked out > Reentered. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |

A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf34); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Append-only arrival/exit events and exact currently-present event roster. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |

## WF35

**Record and review an incident** — S22. Detailed field binding: [FM35](field-dictionary.md#fm35).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Authorized event staff records factual report for a selected event/location/person or an explicitly unlinked parish incident. |
| Data | Initial facts/time/source, immediate real response, involved IDs, restricted evidence, handlers and guardian-disclosure decision. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Recorded > Triaged > Assigned > Follow-up > Resolved > Closed. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |

Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Immediate action follows real approved procedure. Historical reports never receive invented event links. Amendments preserve initial facts; conflict escalates outside implicated authority. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf35); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Restricted incident history with actual actions/follow-up, not a claim emergency broadcasts were delivered. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF35-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF35-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF37

**Reserve, issue and reconcile equipment** — S23. Detailed field binding: [FM37](field-dictionary.md#fm37).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Requester/custodian adds equipment to a booking or starts a standalone loan. |
| Data | Stock/serial, serviceable quantity, overlapping commitments, borrower, issue/due, returned quantity/condition and deposit terms. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Requested > Stock reserved > Issued > Return recorded > Damage/loss reviewed > Closed. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Assigned owner/operators see exact permitted parish/group/record scope. Applicant/guardian receives only their own authorized projection if involved. Clergy/bishop/admin access is field/action-specific; leadership, rank and combined views do not broaden it. Search/exports/counts apply the same restrictions. |
| Ownership and time | Equipment custodian and resource manager. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Partial returns keep outstanding balance. Damage opens WF36 and flags later bookings for alternatives; deposit deductions need approved evidence/refund policy. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf37); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Stock = serviceable available + committed/issued + unavailable with traceable movement history. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF37-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF37-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF37-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF37-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF37-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Bishop or expressly authorized preparation administrator opens New parish. |

| Lifecycle | Private setup draft > Readiness checked > Bishop approved > Created and active > Deactivated > Reactivated or closed. ## WF41

**Count, independently verify and close giving** — S24. Detailed field binding: [FM41](field-dictionary.md#fm41).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Authorized counter opens uniquely identified collection/currency/method batch. |
| Data | Actual USD/LBP lines, denominations where used, envelope/fund allocations, both counter observations, variance and closing revision. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Opened > First count recorded > Independent second count > Variance resolved > Closed and posted. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Entry/approval/payment/reconciliation roles see only granted financial scope; donor identity is a separate grant. Applicant/payer sees own permitted receipts/balance; guardian/household adult has no automatic donor access. Leaders and ordinary office have no full Finance projection. |
| Ownership and time | Treasurer/counting lead; independent counter. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Variance holds batch open. If second person unavailable, remain Unverified and arrange real dual review; any exception requires explicit finance policy, never fake confirmation. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf41); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Locked closing snapshot, actual currency/method totals and uniquely posted movements. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF41-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF41-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF41-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF41-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF41-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF45

**Operate the separate Office Expenses ledger** — S25. Detailed field binding: [FM45](field-dictionary.md#fm45).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Authorized priest/secretary/office administrator records opening balance, allowance or purchase. |
| Data | Actual currency/date/amount, ledger entry type, source-transfer reference, payee/purpose, receipt/voucher and review period. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Draft > Recorded > Evidence reviewed > Monthly reviewed > Locked > Adjustment if needed. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Entry/approval/payment/reconciliation roles see only granted financial scope; donor identity is a separate grant. Applicant/payer sees own permitted receipts/balance; guardian/household adult has no automatic donor access. Leaders and ordinary office have no full Finance projection. |
| Ownership and time | Office ledger custodian; head priest/finance lead approves close. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Unreviewed edit/delete keeps history; after monthly close use reversal/adjustment. Negative balance flags investigation, not invented money. Receipts retain approved access/retention. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf45); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | USD/LBP separate rolling balances and internal vouchers reconcile to canonical transfer/payment references. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF45-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF45-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF45-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF45-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF45-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF46

**Send private conversations and operational notifications** — S26. Detailed field binding: [FM46](field-dictionary.md#fm46).

## WF49

**Define, publish, run and revise a custom workflow** — S28. Detailed field binding: [FM49](field-dictionary.md#fm49).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Authorized process owner opens Forms & workflows then definition or Start run. |
| Data | Versioned form/steps, field audiences, assignees, prerequisites, conditional branches, parallel approvals, deadlines and final outcome. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Definition draft > Published version > Run submitted > Steps in progress > Final step completed > Run closed. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Assigned owner/operators see exact permitted parish/group/record scope. Applicant/guardian receives only their own authorized projection if involved. Clergy/bishop/admin access is field/action-specific; leadership, rank and combined views do not broaden it. Search/exports/counts apply the same restrictions. |
| Ownership and time | Process owner; current step assignee. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Pause blocks new runs by default, existing runs continue; safety stop separately freezes actions. Runs pin version; migration/reopen/cancel preserves prior steps and resource/money consequences. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf49); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Traceable run outcome with version and every actor/time; no false scheduled escalation if runner missing. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF49-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF49-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF49-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF49-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF49-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Named safeguarding/event lead, with real contact and trained operators. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Offline use protected roster/paper log; no verified authority means do not release through ordinary flow. Later reconcile original times/source separately from entry time; no duplicate checkout. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf58); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Actual recipient/exit basis and time recorded, participant removed from event present-roster exactly once. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF58-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF58-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF58-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF58-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF58-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

WF62-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF62-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF62-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF62-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF63

**Keep personal member notes** — S28. Detailed field binding: [FM63](field-dictionary.md#fm63).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Signed-in account opens My notes in permitted personal area of combined home. |
| Data | Author's original title/body, optional private tags, created/updated times and attachment choices. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Draft > Saved private > Edited > Archived > Deleted if retention permits. Unavailable/permission/network states preserve only authorized context; no fictitious decline/financial reversal state. See specific recovery below. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Author only by default. WF48: only explicit task/current-priest share gives named eligible readers access; assignee must see that task. WF63: account author only. Successor/bishop/admin/guardian has no inherited content right. |
| Ownership and time | Account author; platform support cannot browse notebook. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Sign-out clears shared-device sensitive recovery. Conflicts preserve authorized draft. Trash/erase only under approved personal-data schedule, distinct from pastoral/official records. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf63); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Private note persisted only for author, with no accidental staff projection. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF63-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF63-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF63-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF63-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF63-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

| Transition | Source → action → destination | Allowed actor/scope | Prerequisite and confirmation | Committed result and audit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| WF63-T01 | Draft → Record Saved private → Saved private | Author; exact card scope | Account-private, never ordinary office/group/clergy feed. No other household adult or staff role inherits it. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Saved private for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF63-T01 once. |
| WF63-T02 | Saved private → Record Edited → Edited | Author; exact card scope | Account-private, never ordinary office/group/clergy feed. No other household adult or staff role inherits it. Confirmation names record, parish, action and responsible authority; read-only steps do not need an approval dialog. | Commit Edited for the exact owning record and revision; preserve prior state/actual actor/time; update related references specified in the completion rule; emit WF63-T02 once. |

## WF64

**Export permitted data or create an actual backup** — S31 S32. Detailed field binding: [FM64](field-dictionary.md#fm64).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Authorized user selects selected/all-matching export; authorized operator separately invokes documented infrastructure backup procedure. |
| Data | Scope/purpose/columns/date/language, restricted exclusions, snapshot time and actual backup coverage/restore evidence. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Requested > Scope previewed > Authorized > Generated > Downloaded > Audited. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Assigned owner/operators see exact permitted parish/group/record scope. Applicant/guardian receives only their own authorized projection if involved. Clergy/bishop/admin access is field/action-specific; leadership, rank and combined views do not broaden it. Search/exports/counts apply the same restrictions. |
| Ownership and time | Data export owner; separate technical backup custodian. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Role revocation before retrieval blocks export. Partial export reports omitted records; no silent current-page limit. Backup readiness requires real restore exercise, not documentation claim. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf64); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Correct field-limited artifact or explicitly evidenced restorable backup, never conflated. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF64-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF64-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF64-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF64-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF64-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |

## WF65

**Approve formation milestones** — S19. Detailed field binding: [FM65](field-dictionary.md#fm65).

| Card requirement | Proposed behavior |
| --- | --- |
| Entry and trigger | Assigned instructor/leader opens an eligible member milestone on a defined formation program. |
| Data | Program/version, milestones/prerequisites, evidence, attendance where relevant, reviewer and actual completion date. Required/conditional fields, unknown handling and privacy are enumerated in the linked form. |
| Lifecycle | Enrolled > In progress > Evidence submitted > Reviewed > Completed or returned. T90–T93 below define returned-information, resubmission, refusal/cancellation and reviewed correction/reopen branches; only states whose domain permits that branch are eligible. Signed/posted/official terminal states use amendment/reversal, never ordinary cancellation. |
| Transitions | Each numbered row below gives source/action/actor/prerequisite/confirmation/result. Shared transaction contract applies to every row, not only the happy path. |
| Visibility | Applicant/verified guardian or representative: own purpose-scoped projection and explicitly released output. Office: preparation/coordination subset. Priest: assigned clergy decision fields. Bishop: only separately required decision/oversight grant. Leader: own assigned group/event subset. No unrelated donor, medical, pastoral or concern content. |
| Ownership and time | Formation coordinator and designated priest/qualified reviewer. Absent operator is replaced only by an eligible scoped appointee; private case/note rights never move with an ordinary handover. Daily worklist review and defined expiry deadlines apply; no invented always-online promise. |
| Corrections and exceptions | Missing evidence returns milestone only; changed program pins version or reviewed migration. Course certificate is not sacramental certificate. Wrong parish/role or revoked access stops next protected operation; no draft may migrate records across scope. A failed response is checked before retry. |
| Communication | One explicit row for every transition in [notification matrix](notification-matrix.md#wf65); private explanations stay in permitted secure record. No external delivery inferred. |
| End result | Verified program milestone history and clearly labeled formation output. Parent case retains canonical reference and related record links. Only permitted outputs are printable/exportable under P matrix and document rules. |
| Acceptance | WF65-AC1 normal: traverse the stated eligible sequence and verify the exact end result. WF65-AC2 refusal/cancellation: stop before a final result and verify resource/money/access consequences, or exercise unavailable state for pure browsing. WF65-AC3 correction: replace one wrong field/item and prove old version survives. WF65-AC4 boundary: another parish/unassigned actor cannot read or mutate the record, including count/export. WF65-AC5 interruption: drop response after commit and retry same operation; no duplicate effect, no false success. Exercise this card’s specific complication in its linked A scenarios. |
